void *__fastcall operator new(size_t Size)
{
  size_t i; // rbx
  void *result; // rax

  for ( i = Size; ; Size = i )
  {
    result = malloc(Size);
    if ( result != nullptr )
      break;
    if ( callnewh(i) == 0 )
    {
      if ( i != -1 )
        __scrt_throw_std_bad_alloc();
      sub_180024524();
    }
  }
  return result;
}

// FUNC 180023aa4 __scrt_acquire_startup_lock
char _scrt_acquire_startup_lock()
{
  PVOID StackBase; // rcx
  signed __int64 v1; // rax

  if ( (unsigned int)_scrt_is_ucrt_dll_in_use() != 0 )
  {
    StackBase = NtCurrentTeb()->NtTib.StackBase;
    while ( 1 )
    {
      v1 = _InterlockedCompareExchange64(&_scrt_native_startup_lock, (signed __int64)StackBase, 0);
      if ( v1 == 0 )
        break;
      if ( StackBase == (PVOID)v1 )
        return 1;
    }
  }
  return 0;
}

// FUNC 180023ae0 __scrt_dllmain_after_initialize_c
char _scrt_dllmain_after_initialize_c()
{
  _crt_argv_mode startup_argv_mode; // eax

  if ( (unsigned int)_scrt_is_ucrt_dll_in_use() != 0 )
  {
    _isa_available_init();
  }
  else
  {
    startup_argv_mode = get_startup_argv_mode();
    if ( configure_narrow_argv(startup_argv_mode) != 0 )
      return 0;
    initialize_narrow_environment();
  }
  return 1;
}

{
  return (unsigned __int8)_scrt_initialize_onexit_tables(0) != 0;
}

// FUNC 180023b2c __scrt_dllmain_crt_thread_attach
char _scrt_dllmain_crt_thread_attach()
{
  if ( !_acrt_thread_attach() )
    return 0;
  if ( !_acrt_thread_attach() )
  {
    _vcrt_thread_detach();
    return 0;
  }
  return 1;
}
// ENDFUNC
// FUNC 180023b54 __scrt_dllmain_crt_thread_detach
char _scrt_dllmain_crt_thread_detach()
{
  _vcrt_thread_detach();
  _vcrt_thread_detach();
  return 1;
}
// ENDFUNC

        unsigned int ExceptionNum,

    a4(a1, 0, a3);
  return seh_filter_dll(ExceptionNum, ExceptionPtr);
}
// ENDFUNC
// FUNC 180023bcc __scrt_dllmain_uninitialize_c
void _scrt_dllmain_uninitialize_c()
{
  if ( (unsigned int)_scrt_is_ucrt_dll_in_use() != 0 )
  {
    execute_onexit_table(&module_local_atexit_table);
  }
  else if ( is_c_termination_complete() == 0 )
  {
    cexit();
  }
}
// ENDFUNC
// FUNC 180023bfc __scrt_dllmain_uninitialize_critical
__crt_bool _scrt_dllmain_uninitialize_critical()
{
  __crt_bool v0; // cl

  _acrt_uninitialize_critical(false);
  return _acrt_uninitialize_critical(v0);
}
// ENDFUNC
// FUNC 180023c10 __scrt_initialize_crt
char __fastcall _scrt_initialize_crt(int a1)
{
  if ( a1 == 0 )
    is_initialized_as_dll = true;
  _isa_available_init();
  if ( !_acrt_initialize() )
    return 0;
  if ( !_acrt_initialize() )
  {
    _vcrt_uninitialize(false);
    return 0;
  }
  return 1;
}
// ENDFUNC
// FUNC 180023c4c __scrt_initialize_onexit_tables
char __fastcall _scrt_initialize_onexit_tables(unsigned int a1)
{
  if ( !module_local_atexit_table_initialized )
  {
    if ( a1 > 1 )
    {
      _scrt_fastfail(5);
      JUMPOUT(0x180023CD6LL);
    }
    if ( (unsigned int)_scrt_is_ucrt_dll_in_use() == 0 || a1 != 0 )
    {
      *(__int128 *)&module_local_atexit_table._first = _xmm_ffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffff;
      module_local_atexit_table._end = (_PVFV *)-1LL;
      *(__int128 *)&module_local_at_quick_exit_table._first = _xmm_ffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffff;
      module_local_at_quick_exit_table._end = (_PVFV *)-1LL;
